 🌐HAKURI DA JURIYA ONLINE WRITER'S✍

       1⃣➖2⃣

  Wani mutum ne dake zaune akan wata yar karamar katifa,sai kuma wata yarimya wacce bazata wuce shekara goma sha shida bah da take faman shiri da alama makaranta take shirin zuwa

  Kallon ta mutimin yakeyi,cikin muryar tausasawa yake cewa

  "Yanzu Aishatu bazaki yi sauri kizo ki karya kumallo bah?ga lokaci nata wucewa,sai kuma in kinje an dakeki ki fara korafi"

 Da fara'ar ta ta juya tana faman doka murmushi kamar anmata albishir da wani abun 

 "Lokacin ma fah bai gama wucewa bah Abba har zanci abinci nah na tafi kuma ban makara bah"

  "Toh shekenan naji nidai yanzu zo muci sai ki tafi abinki koh?"

  Batare datace komai bah ta karasa wurin abincin,sannan ta dauko musu suka fara cin abincin cikin nutsuwa

 A haka suka gama kammala cin abincin sannan ta tashi ta dauki jakar ta tasaka,kallon Abban nata tayi tace
 
  "Abba zan wuce,
 kayimin Allah ya kiyaye ya kuma bani sa'a"

  "Aishatu kullum a cikin yimiki addu'a nake,saboda haka ki tafi Allah ya kiyaye miki hanya a dawo dake lafiya"

  Da Ameen ta amsa cikin jin dadi ta kama hanya ta fita daga dan dakin nasu

  Tana fita daga dakin nasu sukayi kacibis dawata yarinya wacce bazata wuce shekara goma sha takwas bah,wani irin kallon raini yarinyar take binta dashi sannan tace

  "Wato ke uwar son zuwa makaranta koh?su sauran aiyukan uban waye zai miki su?dazaki wani fito daga cikin akurkin nan wai zakije makaranta?

 "Haba mana Naja'atu duk abinda ya kamata nayi duk nayi fah"

  "Eh lalle idon ki ya bude,dakin nawa fah koh ni zan gyara?"

  "Hmmm!!!toh nidai yanzu na kusan makara sai ki bari inna dawo sai naje na gyara miki"

  Batare data sake tsayawa bah taji abinda zatace bah ta bude yar kofar datake jikin gate din gidan tayi ficewar ta

  Kwafah Naja'atu tayi tare da cewa

 "Lalle yarinyar nan shan ruwanku a gidan nan ya kusa karewa keda uban ki,tunda harni zaki wuce ki bari a tsaye"

  Kama hanya tayi ta nufi cikin gidan nasu kamar zata tashi saama tsabar sauri

  Cikin wani parlour ta shige tanata faman zabga haki kamar wacce tayi aikin karfi
 
  Wata mata ce da alama itace mahaifiyar ta tace

 "Menene kuma haka?kin fita kin dawo kinata faman huci kamar zaki?"

  "Mami wai kiji yarinyar nan inamata magana ta wuce ta barni a tsaye kamar sa'ar ta"

  "Wacce yarinya kenan?"

 Numfashi ta sauke tare da cewa

 "Sannan sakarriyar yarinyar mana!!!Aishatu,wai nace tazo ta gyaramin daki tana cemun wai ta makara zuwa makaranta"

  Wani saurayi ne da bazai wuce shekaru ishirin da takwaas bah yake saukowa daga samam bene yana cewa

  "Gakinan babbar sakarriya,ace dakin nakima sai kin jira anzo an gyara miki tsabar sakalci ya miki yawa da rashin mutunchi"

  "A'a Huzaifah Banason rashin mutuncin nan naka kana jina koh?ita Aishatu dan ubanta batasan meye aikin ta bane a gidan dazata mata haka,ai  koh ubanta yasan aikin shi a gidan nan balle kuma ita"

  "Wai Dan Allah Mami su bah mutane bane?daza'a dinga wulakanta su,dan kawai suna karkashen ku?"

  Cikin jin haushin maganar yaron nata tace

 "Ka wuce ka bani wuri anan run kafin ranka ya baci wallahi"

  Shima haka cikin bacin rai yabar parlour'n tare da watsawa Naja'atu wani irin mugun kallo,na irin zamu hadu din nan

  
  "Ke kuma kidaina bata ranki akan wannan banzan bari ta dawo zata hadu dani"
 
  "Nifah koh Mami sai inga kamar yaya Huzaifa na sonta ne shiyasa daga yaji ana mata fada yake wani zakalkalewa"

  "Allah ya kiyaye dana na soyayya da wannan kucakar yarinyar,mezaiyi da ita?karma ki sake min irin wannan maganar kinji na fadamiki"

  Zunbura baki tayi ta tashi ta nufi dakin ta,a zuciyar ta tana cewa lalle kuwa ashe akwai bidiri dan wallahi nasan yaya Huzaifah son Aishatu yakeyi...!!!

[1/24, 11:52 AM] Deezoh😘😘😘: 🌹🌹🌹NIDA DA ABBA NAH🌹🌹🌹

  *Wannan shafin nakine ki kadai diyar arziki sadiya(chamo) Allah ya albarkaci rayuwar ki,ina godia sosai da addu'a da kuma soyayyar ki a gareni!!!# 1luv🤝*

  🌐HAJOW🌐
🌐HAKURI DA JURIYA ONLINE WRITER'S✍

        3⃣➖4⃣

 Karfe 1:30pm aka tashi daga makaranta,bata tsaya a koina bah sai bakin gate din,kwankwasa gate din tayi tare da cewa

  "Abba nah na dawo"

 Bude mata kofar datake jikin gate din  yayi da fara'ar shi yana cewa 

 "Sannun ki da dawowa Aishatu, kinsha rana koh?"

 "Ai yau babu rana Abba nah,nadai dan gaji ne kadan"

  Daga haka ta  shige dan dakin su dayake kusa da bakin gate din gidan shi kuma ya zauna akan wata yar kujera datake bakin gate

  Cire kayan makarantarta ta farayi bayan tagama tasaka dan hijab dinta sannan ta fito 

 "Abba nah barinje na gyarawa Naja'atu dakin ta dazu ban samu na gyara mata bah na tafi makaranta saboda karna makara,daga nan saina ansu mana abincin"

  "Haba Aishatu!!! Amma dai kinsan fada zasuyi miki sosai koh?meyasa tun daa baki gyara bah?

  "Abba lokacin dana Shiga bata tashi bah shiyasa,fadan su kuma ai nasaba dajin shi"

  Baice mata komai bah itama kuma bata sake cewa komai bah haka ta wuce cikin gidan,shi kuma Abban nata ya girgiza kanshi yana maijin tausayin diyar tashi na yadda rayuwar ta take kasan cewa cikin kaskanci

  Dakin Naja'atu ta wuce kai tsaye,da alama bata cikin dakin dan haka gyara dakin ta farayi  bayan tagama gyarawa ta shiga cikin toilet shima ta wanke shi tas sannan ta fito

  Kitchen din gidan ta nufa da niyyar karbar musu abinci,muryar Mami taji tana cewa kee zonan

  Koh da bata ambaci sunan ta bah tasan cewa ita take kira,dan haka cikin ladabi ta karasa wurin Mami tare da cewa

  "Ina wuni?"

  Harara mai rai da lafiya ta banka  mata sannan tace

 "Da ban wuni bah zaki ganni ne?wato rainin wayon ki har yakai ace Naja'atu na miki magana ki tafi ki barta a wurin koh?"

 "Kiyi hakuri Hajiya lokacin dana zoyin aikin bata tashi bane,bayan kuma ta tashi tace nazo na gyara mata toh ni kuma lokacin zan iya makara zuwa makaranta shiyasa nace ta bari inna dawo zan gyara mata......" 

  "Yimin shiru munafika,yanzu kinje kin gyara mata yanzu koh kuwa?"

 "Eh na gyara"

 "Tashi ki bani wuri algunguma,kafin na haureki da kafa"

  Tashi tayi jiki a sanyaye taje ta ansan musu abincin su saboda anan suke ansa

  Tunda ya hangota tana tahuwa yasan cewa akwai abinda ya faru 
 
  "Abba kazo muci abincin gashinan na anso mana" 
  
  Da toh ya amsa mata sannan ya tashi suka shiga dakin su dan cin abincin

  Wasa kawai takeyi da abincin gaba daya takasa cin abincin,Abban ta ya lura da hakan saboda haka shima ya tsaya da cin abincin,dagowa tayi ta kalli Abban nata sannan tace

 "Abba yaka daina cin abincin badai har ka koshi bah?"

 "Naga kema bakya cine shiyasa nima nadaina ci.Aishatu nasha fadamiki cewa zaman duniya hakuri akeyi,kinga sune masu gidan nan mu aiki mukeyi a karkashin su, saboda haka dole muyi hakuri da abubuwan da zasuyi mana"

 "Abba nah toh meyasa bazamu chanza wani gidan bah?mu fara aikin,inda baza'a dinga wulakanta mu bah!!!dan gaskia ni nafara gajiya da yadda su Naja'atu suke yimaka,gwara ni suyimin amma kai bazan iya cigaba da jure ganin suna wukaqanta ka bah!!!saboda kawai muna karkashin su,ai muma mutane koh?"

 Girgiza kai Abban nata yayi tare da cewa

  "Wata rana sai labari Aishatu,akwai lokacin da duk wannan abubuwan zasu zama labari,saboda haka inaso ki cigaba da danne zuciyar ki ga duk wani abinda zai faru kinji?"

  "Toh naji Abba nah Allah ya kawo mana mafita,amma gaskia mu fara neman wurin wani aikin"

 "Toh naji yanzu dai muci abincin ku kar ya huce"

  Murmushi tayi sannan suka cigaba da cin abincin su har suka gama,sannan ta tashi taje ta mayar da kwanikan,ta dawo ta fara duba littafin ta

        5⃣➖6⃣

      *Asalin labari* 

  A garin bauchi Mallam mustapha shine asalin sunan Abban Aishatu,mallam Mustapha ya kasance yana tare da matar shi guda daya mai suna Habiba suna tare wurin shekaru goma batare da sun sami haihuwa bah ga wani dunbin talauci dayake addabar su watarana mah haka za'a wuni ba'a ci bah kuma ba'a shaba haka

  Basu da wasu yan uwa masu arziki balle suyi tunanin wani zai taimaka musu,kowa acikin su ta kanshi yakeyi!!!

  A cikin shekarar su ta goma da aure Allah ya azurta Habiba da juna biyu,murna a wurin su bata misaltuwa addu'a sukeyi dukkan su akan Allah ya raba lafiya

       *******
  
   Bayan wata tara cif Habiba ta tashi da matsanan ciyar nakuda,ingozuma mallam mustapha yaje ya nemo ta fara  bata agajin gaggawa,amma kuma abun yafi karfin ta

 Samun mallam mustapha ingozumar tayi ta sanar dashi akan su samu su kaita asibiti inba haka bah za'a iya rasa su dukka

  Cikin hanzari suka dauke ta zuwa asibiti,zuwan su asibiti akace baza'a karbe su bah har sai sun ajiye wasu kudi a kasa

 Babu irin rokon da baiyi wa mutanen bah akan zai kawo musu kudin daga baya dan a lokacin bashi da kudin da zai basu amma suka ki karban su

  Tausayin matar ta shi gaba daya ya gama kama shi duk ta gama galabaita,babu yadda zasuyi dan haka ingozuma tace su dauke ta su kaita asibitin gwamnati!!!

  Koh da sukaje chan dinma da kyar suka karbe su,dan cewa sukayi basu kawota da wuri bah gashi duk tagama galabaita,sai kusan bayan awa daya da zuwan su asibitin sannan ta haifu diyar ta 

  Murna a wurin Mallam Mustapha baya faduwa,haka aka sallame su suka koma gida,ranar suna yarinya taci suna *Aishatu* 

  Tun bayan haihuwar Aishatu mahaifiyar ta take ta fama da matsanancin rashin lafiya kullum abin cigaba yakeyi  

  Shekarun Aishatu goma a duniya mahaifiyar ta ta rasu ta barsu ita da Abban ta,rayuwa sukeyi cikin matsi da talauci abincin ci mah wahalar gaske yake musu,haka dai suka cigaba dayin rayuwar su,sai wata irin soyayya mai karfin gaske da take tsakanin su wanda koh suna cikin yunwa kasan cewar su tare na dibe musu wani quncin rayuwa

  Gidan dasuke haya mai gidan yace saidai su tashi kasancewar rashin biyan kudin haya da sukeyi,babu yadda zasuyi haka suka tashi daga gidan suka koma gidan qanin shi,amma kuma saboda yanayin rayuwa zaman ya gagara tsakanin shi da qanin nashi

  Cikin ikon Allah suna cikin haka Allah yasa yasamu aikin gadi ita kuma Aishatu tana shiga gidan tana musu gyaran daki,a gidan *alh.abubakar*,sun dan sami nutsuwa saboda zasu samu suci abinci kuma su kaunta a daki kuma da kudin da ake biyan su Abban ta yake biya mata kudin makaranta, duk da matsin da suke samu a wurin Mami da kuma yarta Naja'atu

  A dayan bangaren kuma Huzaifah shida mahaifin shi indai suna kusa toh bazasu  taba bari a wulaqanta Aishatu bah da mahaifin ya bah hakan yana qara ruru qiyayya a tsakanin su dasu Mami
          ******
   
     *Cigiaban labari*

   Yau ta kama asabar babu makarantar boko sai islamiya,karfe bakwai ta shiga cikin gidan dan gabatar da aiyukan ta

  Da Huzaifah suka hadu yana fitowa daga sashin shi, cikin ladabi ta gaishe shi ya amsa da fara'a!!!har zata wuce ya kira sunan ta juyuwa tayi tana kallon sa

 "Jiya ina fatan dai babu abinda Naja'atu tayi miki dan naji tana qorafin wai baki gyara mata daki bah"

  "A'a babu abinda tayimun dan banma ganta bah tun jiyan"

 "Toh shekenan kije amma kuma inaso zamuyi magana dake in kinsamu lokaci"

  Cikin mamaki take kallon shi dan a iya tunanin ta babu wani abu dazai shiga tsakanin ta dashi,ganin ta tafi wani tunanin na daban yasa yayi murmushi yace

 "Hi!!!karfa ki damu ba wata babbar magana bace nida kaina zanzo muyi maganar,kuma mah a gaban Abban ki zamuyi in kina tsoro ne"

  Gyada kai tayi sannan ta wuce ta barshi wurin,aikin daya kamata tayi ta shiga yi amma kuma gaba daya hankalin ta baya wurin saboda duk wani nazari daza tayi,tayishi amma bata gano wacce magana Huzaifah zaiyi da ita ba kuma har da Abban ta 

  Numfashi taja tare addu'a akan Allah yasa ba wani laifin tayi bah sannan ta fita taje ta shirya zuwa islamiya....!!!

       7⃣➖8⃣

         *********

    Akan dinning suke zaune suna yin breakfasts wani dan dattijo ne ya tsaya da cin abincin daya keyi sannan ya kalli matar datake kusa dashi da alama matar sa ce yace

  "Wai nikam Habib bai dawo gidan nan bane?naga bai fito bah"
 
   Numfasawa matar tayi ta ajiye cokalin dayake hannun ta tare da tashi tana cewa

  "Barin je na duba shi dan nima har na kaunta banji motsin shigowar sa bah!!!"

   Daga haka ta wuce dakin Habib dan ta dubashi 


  Wani saurayi ne kaunce a kasan carpet yanata sharan bacci hankalin shi kaunce,ga takalmi a kafan sa da alama dai anan wurin bacci ya dauke shi

  Ta kusa minti biyar  a tsaye a wurin,duk takaici ya gama cika ta!!!batasan yaza tayi da halin dan nata bah

   Tashin shi ta farayi,a hankali ya gama bude idonsa ya sauke akan mahaifiyar tashi!!!a firgice ya tashi ya zauna dan ganin yadda ya kaunta a wurin kuma yasan mahaifiyar tashi ta gane halin dayake ciki

  "Ina kauna Mummy?"

  "Hmmm!!!!!lafiya lau,wato Habib bazakayi wa kanka fada bah koh?ka cire kanka acikin wannan mummunan rayuwar daka saka kanka acikin ta bah koh?yanzu ka duba ka gani tsabar jaraba dubi a inda ka kaunta,kuma koh  sallah mah bakayi bah daga ganin ka!!!!wai mah karfe nawa ka dawo gidan nan?dan har na kaunta banji shigowar ka bah"

 "Kiyi hakuri Mummy wallahi zan daina na miki alkawari insha Allah!!!ni kaina inaso na daina"

  "Karya kakeyi da kanaso ka daina da tuntuni ka daina,amma bakomai rayuwar kace duk yadda ka zaba haka zata zo maka,Allah dai yasani nida mahaifin ka munyi iya iyawar mu nagani mun sakaka a hanya madaidai ciya amma kaqi.bazan daina rokan maka Allah bah akan ya shirye ka!!!yanzu inka ga dama kaje kayi sallah sai kazo kayi breakfast daddy dinka yana tambayan ka"

  Batare data jira amsar sa bah ta koma kan dinning ta sanarwa da daddy dinshi akan zaiyi wanka gashi nan zuwa,amma kuma kasa cigaba tayi da cin abincin haka ta tashi ta koma cikin parlour ta zauna a zuciyar ta tana rokon Allah ya shirya mata dan ta

  Shima tashin yayi duk jikin shi babu karfi ya wuce toilet,wanka ya farayi sannan yayi alwala ya fito

  Bayan yayi sallah ya shirya cikin qananan kaya ya fito parlour,a zaune ya same daddy da Mummy da kuma kanwar shi Fauziyya

  Gaishe da Daddy din nashi yayi ya amsa batare daya kalle shi bah,gaishe shi Fauziyya itama tayi ya amsa mata,Daddy dinshi ne ya kalle shi yace


  "Jiya karfe nawa ka dawo cikin gidan nan?ban fadamaka inka sake wuce karfe tara a waje karka dawo min gidan nan bah?"

  "Kayi hakuri Daddy wallahi....."

  Batare daya karasa bah Daddy ya dakatar dashi ta hanyar daga mishi Hannu

  "Banaso naji duk abinda zaka fadamin amma wannan yazama kashedin karshe dazan maka,in kuma kana ganin wasa nakeyi ka sake gwada haka ka gani"

  Yana gama fadan haka ya tashi a fusace yabar cikin parlour'n 

 "Kaji dadi koh Habib?duk hukuncin da mahaifin ka ya dauka akanka toh ka kuka da kanka bada kowa bah,dan babu abinda zanyi akai"

 Itama tashin tayi ta barshi a wurin shida Fauziyya,dafi kanshi yayi yana dana sanin abinda ya jefa kanshi a cikin

 "Bross na kawo maka breakfast dinka nan ne koh zakaje chan kayi?"

 "A'a ki barshi kawai na koshi banajin yunwan kuma"

 Batare daya tsaya yaji mai zatace bah ya tashi ya koma cikin dakin shi ya kaunta 

     *Wanene Habib*

   Habib Usman Isa shine cikakken sunan shi,a yanzu haka bazai wuce shekaru 29 bah a duniya, a inda yake da qanwar sa daya mai shekaru 20 a duniya,sun tasu cikin so da kauna a wurin iyayen su

  Iyayen su masu arzikin gaske ne amma hakan baisa sun kasa bawa yaran su tarbiyyar data dace bah,bayan Habib ya gama secondary dinshi ya bukaci mahaifin shi ya kaishi kasar waje yayi degree dinshi a chan

 Amma mahaifin nashi yace a'a,da kyar dai ya yadda ya turashi chan karatun dan karantan engineering,duk wani abu dazai bukata mahaifin nashi yana  tura mishi

 Tunda yaje ya hadu da lalatattun abokanai inda suka koya mishi shaye-shaye kala-kala,tun baya yadda yayi har yakai shida kanshi zaije ya sayu yasha abinshi,amma kuma haka bai hanashi yin karatun shi bah yadda ya dace dan bashi da burin daya wuce yaga yazama engineer
  
 Yakan zo Hutu amma iyayen shi basu gane cewa yana shaye-shaye bah saboda a boye yakeyi,sai bayan ya gama karatunshi ya dawo sannan suka gane yana shaye-shaye sunyi matukar bakin ciki gashi abin ya fara zama a jikin shi zaiyi wuya ya daina a lokaci kusa

     *Cigaban labari*

   Tunda taje islamiya take tunanin abinda Huzaifah zai cemusu itada Abban ta,amma takasa gane komai,haka aka tashi ta dawo gida

  Bayan ta cire kayanta taje ta dauko musu abincin su,sun zauna suna ci Abban ta ya gane akwai abinda yake damunta

 "Aishatu menene kuma ya faru?na fadamaki ki daina sakawa kanki damuwa bashi da wani amfani dazai mana"

  "Abba babu komai,wani Abu ne yake daure mun kai!!!Huzaifah ne yace wai zaizo muyi magana nidashi da kai,toh shine nake ta tunanin wacce irin magana ce wannan"

  "Shi Huzaifa ne da akanshi yace miki hakan"

  Gyada kanta tayi tare da cewa

 "Eh haka yace mun shine na kasa gane dalilin shi na cewa hakan"

 Ajiyar zuciya Abban nata yayi sannan yace

  "Insha Allah babu komai sai alkhairi,karki damu bari inyazo sai muji mai zaice"

  Da toh ta amsa sannan suka cigaba da cin abincin nasu kowannen su yana tunanin mai Huzaifah zaice musu.

       9⃣➖1⃣0⃣

   Yau kauna biyu kenan da Huzaifah yacewa Aishatu zasuyi magana amma shiru koh haduwa basu sakeyi bah

  Kamar kullum bayan ta gama aiyukanta ta shirya ta wuce makaranta,duk tsawon kaunakin nan tunaninta bai wuce wacce irin magana zasuyi da Huzaifah bah

  Har lokacin tashi yayi aka tashe su,tana cikin tafiya a nutse ta jiyo horn din mota batare data juya bah ta matsa daga gefen dataji karan ya fito

  Sunanta taji an kira hakan yasa ta juya dan taga waye,Huzaifah tagani a cikin mota yana mata murmushi!!!tsayawa tayi a wurin tana tunanin me kuma ya biyo dashi tanan
 
  Har ya karaso kusa da ita bata sani bah,dariya tasu ta bashi ganin yadda lokaci daya yanayin ta ya chanza

  "Wai ke meyasa kika fiyi tsoro ne?koh kuma baki ganeni bane?"

  Murmushin dole tayi sannan tace

  "Mezai hanani gane ka kuma?kawai dai banyi tsammanin ganin ka anan bane"

 "Toh shekenan ki shigo mu karasa gida kinga rana tayi yawa"

 "A'a ka barshi zan karasa ai na kusa mah"

 "Aishatu banason musu,nace ki shigo mu tafi!!koh kuma baki yadda dani bane"

  Cikin sauri ta girgiza kanta tare da cewa

 "A'a ba haka nake nufi bah!!!banaso hajiya ta gan....."

  Dakatar da ita yayi ta hanyar cewa

 "Nidai nace kizo mu tafi,in kuma bazaki shiga bah toh nima barin sauko mu karasa tare,in yaso anjima nazo na dauki motar"

 Bayan motar ta bude zata shiga amma yace saidai ta shiga gaba,babu yadda zatayi dan bata cika son magana da yawa bah haka ta bude gaban motar ta shiga 

  Gaba daya duk a tsorace take ta kasa sake jikin ta,haka suka isa layin gidan,tana ganin sun shiga layin gidan ta kalle shi tare da cewa

  "Dan Allah ka tsaya na sauka anan sai na karasa"

 Yi yayi kamar mah baisan tayi magana bah balle kuma yaji me tace,haka suka isa bakin gate!!!horn yayi Abban ta yazo ya bude musu,bayan ya gama parking ta fito da sauri gudun kar wani ya ganta duk da tasan Abban ta ya gansu tare

  Amma ina abunda take gudu saida ya faru,tana fitowa suka hada ido da Naja'atu!!!wani irin kallo ta bita dashi na tuhuma,ita kuwa da sauri ta wuce cikin dakin su

  Shima fitowar yayi ganin yadda Naja'atu take kallon shi yasa ya daga mata gira tare da cewa

 "Lafiya kuwa?wannan wane irin kallo ne haka?"

  Murmushin mugunta tayi sannan tace

  "A'a babu komai ganin abinda ban saba gani bah nayi shiyasa nake mamaki"

 "Toh kima daina mamaki dan yanzu kika fara ganin haka,inma baki saba gani bah toh ki shirya zaki cigaba da ganin hakan har sai kin saba kin daina mamakin hakan,ina fata kin gane kuma kin fahimce ni sosai"

 Ba tare daya sake sauraron ta bah ya wuce ya barta a tsaye a wurin,shima Abban Aishatu duk jikin shi yayi sanyi dan yasan dole akwai matsalar dazata biyo baya!!!shima dakin nasu ya shiga ya sameta ta zabga uban tagumi,karasawa yayi ya cire mata tagumin sannan yace

 "Aishatu ya akayi haka?meya hadaki da shiga motar Huzaifah?gashi naga Naja'atu taga fitowar ki daga cikin motar,kuma kinsan dole Hajiya taji wannan maganar koh?"

 "Abba nima nakasa gane mai Huzaifah yake nufi,bayan an tashi mu daga makaranta muka hadu dashi a hanya ya matsamin akan saina shiga motar min karaso,mun shigo layin nan nace ya sauke ni na karaso amma yayi kamar bai jini bah"

  Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke sannan yace 

 "Nan gaba karki sake shiga motar sa kinji koh?yanzun mah nasan bamu tsira bah mujira muga mai Hajiya zata mana gashi Alhaji bayanan"

  Naja'atu na shiga cikin gidan ta wuce dakin Mami,zaunawa tayi a gefen gado tana jiran Mami tagama wayar datake yi

 "Naja'atu meya faru?dan daga ganin ki nasan akwai magana a bakin ki"

  Gyara zaman ta tayi sannan tace

 "Mami badai ina cemiki yaya Huzaifah nason Aishatu bah kinki yadda bah koh?toh yau maganata ta fara fitowa,yanzun nan ya sauke ta a motar shi da alama daga makaranta ya dauko ta"

 "Da gaske kikeyi koh kuma wasa?"

 "Wallahi da gaske nakeyi,in kuma baki yadda bah ki tambaye su kiji"

 "Kyalesu dani zan kamasu a hanuna nan bah da dadewa bah,sai sunyi dana sanin haduwar su  damu balle kuma har suyi tunanin zasu sami Huzaifah,yanzu kiyi shirun ki koh nuna musu nasan zancen bazan yi bah"

 Har taje ta dauko musu abincin rana bataji Mami tace komai,haka mah da daddare taje ta dauko musu nan ma batace mata kala bah,ji tayi tadan samu sauki na jin Mami batace mata komai bah

  Suna cikin cin abincin sukaji sallamar Huzaifah a bakin kofar dakin su,gaban tane ya fadi sabods tagane cewa muryar shice,Abban nata ne yayi karfin halin amsa masa sallamar tasa

 Shigowa yayi yasamu wuri ya zauna ya gaida Abba,amsawa yayi cikin fara'a tare da cewa

  "Malalam Huzaifah Allah dai yasa lafiya?naganka a wannan lokacin"

  Murmushi yayi sannan yace 

 "Yace lafiya lau Abba,dama nazo ne inaso ka bani izinin neman auren Aishatu in babu damuwa,daga Alhaji ya kawo za'a tsayar da magana"

  Ba ita bama har Abban nata saida wutar kanshi ta fara daukewa,bakin ta na rawa tace

  "Dan Allah karka mana haka *nida Abba nah* Kai kasan wannan abun bamai yiwuwa bane,kabari mu cigaba da zama a inda Allah ya ajiye mu"

  "Aishatu ki yarda dani karki qi amincewa da bukata ta,nasan abinda kuke tsoro amma ina mai tabbatar muku da babu abinda zai faru sai alheri insha Allah!!!Abba bakace komai bah Dan Allah karka cemin a'a"

  "Huzaifah ni a halin yanzu bansan mah mai zance maka bah!!amma sanin kanka ne koh da na amince Aishatu mah ta amince toh iyayen ka bazasu amince bah,karfa kamanta matsayin mu a cikin gidan nan"

  "Ina sane da matsayin ku,amma koma mutane ne kamar kowa kunada ikon yin rayuwa kamar  yadda duk wani dan Adam zaiyi,maganar iyaye nah kuma nasan mahaifi nah baida matsala kuma kaima kasani,toh indai nasamu goyon baya daga wurinshi toh banida matsala,nasan ma zan samu,yanzu nidai amincewar ku kawai nake bukata"

 Shiru dukkan su sukayi babu wanda ya iya qara cewa komai,saida ya sake cewa

 "Dan Allah Abba karki qini ku kaunace ni kamar yadda ka kaunace ku,karku cemin a'a ku bani dama akan hakan"

  "Shekenan Huzaifah!!!mun gode da irin son daka nuna mana idan kun fahimci juna da Aishatu babu matsala Allah ubangiji yasa hakan yazama mana alkhairi baki dayan mu"

  Cikin farin ciki ya amsa da Ameeen sannan ya musu sallama ya fita daga dakin,cikin mamaki Aishatu ta kalle mahaifin nata tace

   Chan yamma  gajiya tayi da zaman ita kadai,sallah ce kawai take tayar da ita,dan haka ta tashi ta fara zagaye cikin gidan,gida ne sosai na alfarmah,kallon gidan takeyi cike da shauki
 
  Yunwar data fara jine yasa ta koma ciki,tunanin abinda zata girka ta shiga yi,yanke shawaran girka jollof rice tayi,dan haka ta fara kici-kicin yin ta

  Dab da magrib ta kammala,juyewa tayi a cikin cooler sannan ta koma daki ta gabatar da sallah,anan ta zauna har saida akayi sallar isha'i sannan ta fito, zubawa kanta abincin dazata iya cinyewa tayi

  Ba wani iya cin abincin tayi bah sai faman tunanin data saka a gaba,haka ta gama ta tattara wurin sannan ta koma cikin dakin ta

  Wanka ta shiga tayi,tana fitowa ta saka kayan bacci ta haye kan gado duk da tasan cewa bawai baccin zatayi bah,gashi babu wani abu dazata yi ya dauke mata kiwar da take ciki

  So takeyi mah taga Abban ta koh kuma taji muryan shi,amma koh waya babu a hanun ta balle ta kira Fauziyya ta kaiwa Abban nata,tana cikin tunanin oga Habib ya bankado kofa ya shigo

   A buge yake sosai, a zabure tatashi tana kallon yadda yake tangadi kana ganinshi kasan bashi da wani kuzari a jikin shi

 A wurin ya tsuguna ta shiga kwararu amai,Aishatu kuwa inbanda kuka babu abinda takeyi sai kallon yadda yake yin amai,bayan ya gama ya mike a wurin tuni bacci yayi awon gaba dashi

  Share hawayenta tayi ta sauko ta fara aikin gyara wurin daya bata,bayan ta gama ta cire mishi takalmin daya ke kafan shi tare da cire mishi rigan jikin shi dan shima amai ya fara batawa

  Bazata iya kai shi dakin shi bah yau,dan haka ta tallabo shi ta kwantar dashi akan gadon ta tare da rufa mishi bargo,wani pillow din ta dauka ta koma parlour

   Kuka takeyi sosai,ga kiwan Abban ta datake yi,koh ba komai da suna tare shi zai lallashe ta,amma yau kam babu mai lallashin ta,haka ta dinga dirzar kukan ta har baccin hawala ya dauke ta.

 "Kullum ina tunanin ku,na yau ne kawai yafi yawa,yasu Mummy dasu Abba"

  "Lafiyan su qalau sunce  gaishe ki"

  "Ayya ina amsawa"

  "Sis wai meya faru ne?naga sai kallon nah kikeyi"

  Ajiye cup din dayake hannun ta tayi ta riko hannun Aishatu tare da cewa

  "Indai da gaske kin dauke ni a matsayin sis dinki da gaske,toh ki fadamin abinda yake daminki,in kuma bazaki fadamin bah toh daga yau karki sake kira nah da sis dinki,kuma bazan sake zuwa gidan nan bah"

  "Haba sis yazaki dinga fadan haka,bani da wata Yar uwa data wuce ki fah,ni babu abinda yake damunah"

  Tashi Fauziyya tayi ranta a bace ta shiga yafa gyalen ta sannan ta dauki jakar ta da alama tafiya zatayi,saurin tsayar da ita Aishatu tayi tana cewa

  "Ina kuma zaki?"

  "Ki sakeni bansani bah,na dauka da gaske kin dauke ni a matsayin yar uwarki ashe bah haka bane,tinda har bazaki iya fadamin damuwar ki bah"

  Tuni hawaye ya fara zubuwa Aishatu,hannun ta Fauziyya ta riko tace

  "Yar uwa zata iya yiwa yar uwar ta maganin matsalar datake ciki,saboda haka ki fadamin koh shawara ne saina baki"

  "Zan fadamiki amma sai kinmun alqawarin bazaki fadawa kowa bah,dan banason kowa ya sani"

  "Naji bazan fadawa kowa bah,insha Allah!!!yanzu ki fadamin menene ke damun ki kuma karki boye mun komai"

   Share hawayen ta tayi sannan ta fara bawa Fauziyya labarin abubuwan da suke faruwa tin daga ranar da'aka kawota a matsayin matar Habib har kawo yau

  Sosai Fauziyya ta tausayawa Aishatu tare da jinjinawa hakuri irin nata

  "Amma Aishatu shine bazaki fadawa kowa bah?saiki zauna a haka,har sai bakin cikin shi ya kashe ki kenan koh kome,kuma kiyi hakuri bazan iya boyewa Mummy maganar nan bah dole ta sani dan a dauki mataki"

  "Karki mun haka,kin riga da kinyi alqawari fah"

  "Kiyi hakuri qanwata dole zan karya alqawarin nan"

  Bata tsaya jin abinda Aishatu zatace bah ta fice daga parlour'n ta kama hanyar fita,cikin sauri Aishatu ta bita tana kiran ta amma taki tsayawa haka ta fice daga gidan ta kama hanyar komawa gida ranta a bace

  "Da sauki kaina ne kawai yake dan yimun ciwo amma shima ba kamar jiya bah"

  Biyan kudin sukayi sannan suka fito a tare dan komawa gida,sam Aishatu bata lura da Habib dayake tsaye bah a jikin motar shi,saida taji muryar shi yana cewa

  "Hajiya kizo mu koma gida"

  Da sauri ta dago tana kallon shi da mamaki sannan ta juya tana kallon Fauziyya,motar su datake tsaye tana jiran su Aishatu ta nufa da niyyan shiga,cikin sauri Habib yashaa gaban ta tare da riko hannun ta yana cewa

  "Ki rufawa kanki asiri kizo mu tafi,kindai san ke mai laifi ce koh?kinyi tafiya bada izini nah bah sannan yanzu kin fito batare da izini nah bah,saboda haka kizo mu tafi in bah haka bah chak zan dagaki anan nasaka ki mota"

  Takaici yau kam ta gama shanshi,dubawa tayi taga da mutane a wurin kuma tasan zai iyayin abinda yace tunda bah kunya bace dashi,hakan yasa ta bishi,bude gaban motor yayi ta shige sannan ya dawo wurin Fauziyya

  "Sis kuje gida muma muna tahowa"

  "Amma dai kasan....."

  "Karki ce komai babu inda zan kaita gidan muma zamu dawo,muna bayan ku"

  Gyada kai tayi sannan tacewa Naja'atu tazo su tafi,shima motar shi ya koma ya shige tare da tayar a ita suka kama hanya,tayi tunanin wani wurin zai wuce da ita amma kuma saita ga gida suka nufa

  Har suka kai gida batace mishi komai bah shima kuma baice mata kala bah,kusan tare suka shiga gidan dasu Fauziyya,bayan yayi parking tafara kokarin budewa danta fita,amma kofar a rufe,juyuwa tayi tana cewa

  "Mallam ka bude min koh?"

  Murmushi yayi tare da cewa

  "Innaqi fah?bakimin godiya bah bakimun komai bah zaki fita?sannan naga alama kin rainani koh?"

  "Allah ka budemun, kuma sai na fadawa Mummy"

  "Toh menene inkin fadamata?nidai yanzu ki fadamin yaushe zamu koma gida?dan nagaji da zama anan gaskiya"


  Tuni kwalla ta cika mata ido,cikin rawar murya tace
 
  "Dan Allah ka budemin na tafi"

  "Amma kinada matsala,toh miye abin kukan kuma?in baki daina kukan nan bah wallahi bazan bude miki bah kuma yanzu zamu wuce"

  Cikin sauri ta fara share kwallan da fara zubo mata,dariya taso ta bashi hakan yasa ya bude mata ai da sauri ta fice hakan ya bashi daman yin dariyar shi a fili yace

  "Aishatu insha Allah kin daina yin kuka"

   Haka shima ya fito ya wuce cikin gidan yana mai jin dadi a ransa.

      *Bayan kwana biyu*

  Biki yayi biki inda aka saka amarya a lalle sannan akayi walima da sauran su

  Da mismisalin karfe 1:30pm dunbin jama'a suka shaida auren *Huzaifah da Fauziyya* mutane a wurin ba'a cewa komai dan kowa yasamu halartar jama'u da yawan gaske

  Tun bayan sallar magrib aka fara shirin zuwa wurin dinner kamar yadda suka tsara,mai makeup har gida tazo dan tayiwa amare kwalliya

  Tabbas kowa amare sun sha kwalliya ta gani a fada,abin sha'awa dasu kamar ka sace su,haka aka fara tafiya wurin daya bayan daya inda aka rage daga amarya da Aishatu sai kuma Mummy wanda dama ita Aishatu ke jira 

  Aishatu ce ta fito da amarya ta sata a mota sannan ta dawo tacewa Mummy tazo su tafi,haka Mummy ta fito dan su tafi,suna fitowa suka hadu da Habib,kallon shi Mummy yayi ta watsar shi kowa yayi saurin cewa

  "Mummy kuzo mu tafi mu kadai muka rage"

  "Ka tafi abinka zamu taho"

  "Dan Allah Mummy kizo mu tafi"

  Harara ta balla mishi ta riko hannun Aishatu suka wuce motar datake jiransi,haka suka shige suka barshi a tsaye anan,shafa kanshi yayi shima ya shige motor shi yabi bayan su

  Koh da suka isa wurin har an fara gabatar da shirye-shiyen dan haka suma suka shige bikin ya cigaba da gudana yadda ya kamata

 Duk Inda Aishatu tayu idon Habib na kanta,chan ya hango wani ya tsareta yana mata magana,ita kuwa sai gaba takeyi,cikin zafin nama ya taso zuciyar shi na mishi zafi,gefe chan ya ture wanda yake binta ita kuma ya kamo hanun ta ya nufi hanyar fita da ita

  Turjewa takeyi tana fadin ya sake ta amma yaqi,sai yi dayayi kamar baya jinta,motar ya bude ya sakata shima ya zagaya ya shiga,tayar da motor yayi suka fice a wurin

  Hanyar gidan shi taga ya nufa ai tini ta fashe da kuka bah tsayawa,har cikin ransa yake jin kukan nan nata hakan yasa yayi parking yana cewa

  "Aishatu dan Allah kiyi shiru,wallahi banson kukan nan"

  "Toh ka maidani in bakaso"

  "Bazan iya maida ke bah,hakuri nah ya gama karewa nayi iya dauriyar da zan iyayi kiyi shiru dan Allah"

  Ai kamar yace ta kara sautin kukan nata,yasan bazatayi shiru hakan yasa ya sake tayar da motar suka nufi gida,dan harga Allah bayajin zai iya maida ita a cigaba da kallon mishi ita

  Har suka isa bata daina kukan bah,bayan yayi parking ya fito sannan itama ya fito da ita suka shiga cikin gidan,kai tsaye dakin shi ya wuce da ita har zuwa lokacin bata daina kukan bah 

  Jikin shi ya jawota tsam ya rugumeta yana faman lallashi amma bata daina kukan bah,jinta a jikin shi yasa ya fara fita a hayyacin sa nan da nan ya fara mata sababbin al'amari,ture shi ta shiga yi har karfin ta ya kare kuma tana dan jin dadin abinda yake mata,dole haka ta kyale shi mai afkuwa ta afku

  Wannan karan shi kanshi yaji dadin kusan tanta dayayi,itama kuma bazata ce bataji dadi bah,da kanshi ya hada mata ruwan wanka yace taje tayi,haka ta tashi ta shiga dan tayi wankan shi koma ya wuce dakin ta dan ya kawo mata kaya

  Bayan ta fito ta saka kayan daya dauko mata,shi kuma ya shige dan yayi wankan,kafin ya fito bacci har ya dauke ta,kallonta ya tsaya yi sannan shima yaje ya shirya yazo ya gyara mata kwanciyar ta sannan ya kwanta

  Bai iyayin baccin bah wani irin dadi yakeji a ransa yau,bangaren su Mummy kuwa anzo ana hoto nah amma bataga Aishatu bah,hakan yasa ta fara dubata amma babu ita babu alamarta

  Chan hankalin ta ya tafi wurin Habib shima kuma baya nan,hakan yasa tasa a ranta Habib ne ya tafi da ita dan tasan sarai zai iya.

    *Washe gari*

  Shiya fara tashi yaje yayi alwala sannan yazo ya tashe ta,saida ya jirata sannan sukayi sallar a tare,mamakin shi takeji sosai,tana cikin tunanin nan taji ya riko mata hannu yana cewa

  "Aishatu dan Allah ki daure ki yafewa wannan mijin naki,abubuwa da yawa sun faru amma kiyi hakuri karki gujeni,wallahi bazan iya zama babu kee bah"

  Shiru tayi ta kasa cemishi komai sai hawayen dayake zubo mata,hannun shi yasa yana share mata tare da rungumeta yana cewa

  "Dan Allah ki daina kukan nan haka wallahi har cikin raina nake jin shi"

  Babu laifi ta sassauta kukan nata haka har wani baccin ya dauke ta,shima baisan lokacin da baccin ya dauke shi bah,ringing din wayar shi ta farkar dashi,ganin Mummy  ce yasa ya tashi ya zauna tare da daukan wayan

  "Habib waye ya baka izinin daukan Aishatu?"

  "Mummy dan Allah kiyi hakuri ki barmin matata,wallahi na wahala da yawa,nayi miki alqwarin zan kula da ita yadda ya kamata"

  Jin magana sama-sama ne yasa itama ta tashi,mummy bata ce komai ta kashe wayar ta tana mai godiya ga allah,kallon Aishatu yayi ya sakar mata murmushi

  "Nidai dan Allah ka maidani gida"

  "Wanne gida zan maidake,bakya jin kunya ace kin gudu da mijin ki yanzu na kaiki wurin su?"

  Turo baki tayi zatayi magana yayi saurin katseta da cewa

  "Kiyi shiru pls!!ki bari zuwa jibi zan kaiki kafin nan mutane sun tafi,kinji koh?"

 Babu yadda zatayi hakan yasa ta gyada mishi kanta,haka dai ranar suka wuni da ita sai nan-nan yake yi da ita

  "Yaya Habib shine zaka dauke ta ku tafi bamuyi sallama bah koh"

  "Yi hakrui sis,zan kawo miki ita insha Allah,hakurin yayan ki ne ya kare"

  "Toh naji Allah yasa bani ita bamuyi magana"

  "Hello!!!sis ya kike?"

  "Bansani bah,wato ke me miji koh?shine zaki kama hanya ki tafi baki fadamin bah koh?"

  "Yi hakuri bah laifi nah bane wallahi"

  "Hmm!!!my sis gaskia inajin dadi,Allah ya baku zaman lafiya mai dorewa da yaya nah"

  Da ameen Aishatu ta amsa haka suka dan taba hira,sannan daga bisani suka kashe wayar.

     *Bayan wata goma sha daya*


   Haka zaman Aishatu da Habib ya kasance cike da kulawa da kaunar juna,babu kamar Habib,baya kaunar yaga abinda zai taba mishi matar shi

 Soyayya ta ban mamaki suke sha a 'yan watannin na,inda anan ne har Aishatu ta haifi danta wanda aka mayar mishi da sunan abban ta wato *Mustapha* suna kiranshi da Muhammad

  Murna a wirin su ba'a cewa komai mussamman Aishatu da aka saka mata sunan Abban ta,itama Fauziyya tana nan dauke da tsohon cikin haihuwa koh yau koh gobe

  Akai-akai suke zuwa ziyar tar su Mummy,Inda Aishatu ta rubuta jarabawar ta ta weac ta samu nasarar cinyeta yanzu haka zata rubuta jamb Inda zata karanta pharmacy.
